i < dim; ++i) {\n        double t = 0.0;\n        for (index_t j = rowDelimiters[i]; j < rowDelimiters[i + 1]; ++j) {\n            const auto col = cols[j];\n            t += val[j] * vec[col];\n        }\n        out[i] = t;\n    }\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: verifyResults\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Verifies correctness of results by comparing to reference solution\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   reference: array holding the reference result vector\n//   result: array holding the result vector to verify\n//   size: number of elements per vector\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nbool verifyResults(const double* reference, const double* result, const index_t size) {\n    for (index_t i = 0; i < size; ++i) {\n        const double ref = reference[i];\n        const double res = result[i];\n        if (std::abs(ref) < 1e-10) {\n            // For very small values, check absolute error\n            if (std::abs(res) > MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR) {\n                printf(\"Validation failed at index %u: reference %.10e, got %.10e\\n\", i, ref, res);\n                return false;\n            }\n        } else {\n            // Check relative error\n            const double relError = std::abs((res - ref) / ref);\n            if (relError > MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR) {\n                printf(\"Validation failed at index %u: reference %.10e, got %.10e (rel error: %.10e)\\n\",\n                       i, ref, res, relError);\n                return false;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of rows/columns in the matrix (default: 1024)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -s <num>     Sparsity: 1 out of N entries is non-zero (default: 10)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -i <num>     Number of iterations (default: 10)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -m <val>     Maximum value for matrix and vector elements (default: 1.0)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    index_t numRows = 1024;\n    index_t sparsity = 10;\n    index_t iterations = 10;\n    double maxVal = 1.0;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n\n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            numRows = static_cast<index_t>(atoi(argv[++i]));\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-s\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            sparsity = static_cast<index_t>(atoi(argv[++i]));\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-i\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            iterations = static_cast<index_t>(atoi(argv[++i]));\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-m\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            maxVal = atof(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Calculate number of non-zero elements\n    const index_t nItems = (numRows * numRows) / sparsity;\n\n    printf(\"Sparse Matrix-Vector Multiplication (SpMV) Benchmark\\n\");\n    printf(\"Matrix size: %u x %u\\n\", numRows, numRows);\n    printf(\"Sparsity: 1 out of %u entries is non-zero\\n\", sparsity);\n    printf(\"Non-zero elements: %u (%.2f%% sparse)\\n\", \n           nItems, 100.0 * (1.0 - static_cast<double>(nItems) / (numRows * numRows)));\n    printf(\"Iterations: %u\\n\", iterations);\n    printf(\"Max value: %.2f\\n\", maxVal);\n    printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n\n    // Allocate and initialize data structures\n    std::vector<double> h_val(nItems);                  // Non-zero values\n    std::vector<index_t> h_cols(nItems);                // Column indices\n    std::vector<index_t> h_rowDelimiters(numRows + 1);  // Row delimiters\n    std::vector<double> h_vec(numRows);                 // Dense vector\n    std::vector<double> h_out(numRows);                 // Output vector\n\n    printf(\"Initializing data structures...\\n\");\n    fill(h_vec.data(), numRows, maxVal);\n    fill(h_val.data(), nItems, maxVal);\n    initRandomMatrix(h_cols.data(), h_rowDelimiters.data(), nItems, numRows);\n\n    // For validation, compute reference solution\n    std::vector<double> h_reference;\n    if (validate) {\n        printf(\"Computing reference solution...\\n\");\n        h_reference.resize(numRows);\n        spmvCpu(h_val.data(), h_cols.data(), h_rowDelimiters.data(), \n                h_vec.data(), numRows, h_reference.data());\n    }\n\n    // Perform SpMV computation\n    printf(\"Computing SpMV...\\n\");\n    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n\n    for (index_t iter = 0; iter < iterations; ++iter) {\n        spmvCpu(h_val.data(), h_cols.data(), h_rowDelimiters.data(),\n                h_vec.data(), numRows, h_out.data());\n    }\n\n    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);\n\n    printf(\"Computation time: %ld ms\\n\", duration.count());\n    \n    // Calculate performance metrics\n    const double gflops = (2.0 * nItems * iterations) / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e9;\n    const double avgTime = duration.count() / static_cast<double>(iterations);\n    \n    printf(\"Average time per iteration: %.3f ms\\n\", avgTime);\n    printf(\"Performance: %.3f GFLOPS\\n\", gflops);\n    \n    // Print results for external validation\n    if (printResults) {\n        print_results(h_out, \"OutputVector\");\n    }\n\n    // Validation\n    if (validate) {\n        printf(\"Validating result...\\n\");\n        const bool valid = verifyResults(h_reference.data(), h_out.data(), numRows);\n\n        if (valid) {\n            printf(\"Validation: PASSED\\n\");\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Validation: FAILED\\n\");\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    return 0;\n}\nCuda compilation tools, release 12.0, V12.0.140\nBuild cuda_12.0.r12.0/compiler.32267302_0\nmpirun (Open MPI) 4.1.6\n256","stderr":"","interrupted":false,"isImage":false,"noOutputExpected":false}}

longer need their host copies\n    l_rowDelimitersBuf = {};\n    l_colsBuf = {};\n    l_valBuf = {};\n\n    // For validation, compute reference solution\n    std::vector<double> h_reference;\n    if (validate && root) {\n        printf(\"Computing reference solution...\\n\");\n        h_reference.resize(numRows);\n        spmvCpu(h_val.data(), h_cols.data(), h_rowDelimiters.data(),\n                h_vec.data(), numRows, h_reference.data());\n    }\n\n    // Gather layout (row counts per rank fit in int since numRows < 2^32 and\n    // rows are bounded by the index type)\n    std::vector<int> recvCounts(nranks), displs(nranks);\n    for (int r = 0; r < nranks; ++r) {\n        displs[r] = static_cast<int>(workerRowBounds[firstWorker[r]]);\n        recvCounts[r] = static_cast<int>(workerRowBounds[firstWorker[r + 1]] - workerRowBounds[firstWorker[r]]);\n    }\n\n    // Perform SpMV computation\n    if (root) printf(\"Computing SpMV...\\n\");\n    MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n\n#pragma omp parallel for num_threads(myGpus) schedule(static, 1)\n    for (int g = 0; g < myGpus; ++g) {\n        GpuWorker& w = workers[g];\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaSetDevice(w.device));\n        for (index_t iter = 0; iter < iterations; ++iter) {\n            spmvGpu(w.d_val, w.d_cols, w.d_rowDelimiters, w.d_vec, w.rows, w.avgNnz, w.d_out, w.stream);\n        }\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());\n        if (w.rows > 0) {\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(l_out.data() + (w.rowBegin - myRowBegin), w.d_out,\n                                       static_cast<size_t>(w.rows) * sizeof(double),\n                                       cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, w.stream));\n        }\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(w.stream));\n    }\n\n    MPI_Gatherv(l_out.data(), static_cast<int>(myRows), MPI_DOUBLE,\n                root ? h_out.data() : nullptr, recvCounts.data(), displs.data(), MPI_DOUBLE,\n                0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n\n    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);\n\n    // Release GPU resources\n    for (GpuWorker& w : workers) {\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaSetDevice(w.device));\n        cudaFree(w.d_val);\n        cudaFree(w.d_cols);\n        cudaFree(w.d_rowDelimiters);\n        cudaFree(w.d_vec);\n        cudaFree(w.d_out);\n        cudaStreamDestroy(w.stream);\n    }\n    cudaHostUnregister(l_out.data());\n\n    int exitCode = 0;\n    if (root) {\n        printf(\"Computation time: %ld ms\\n\", duration.count());\n\n        // Calculate performance metrics\n        const double gflops = (2.0 * nItems * iterations) / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e9;\n        const double avgTime = duration.count() / static_cast<double>(iterations);\n\n        printf(\"Average time per iteration: %.3f ms\\n\", avgTime);\n        printf(\"Performance: %.3f GFLOPS\\n\", gflops);\n\n        // Print results for external validation\n        if (printResults) {\n            print_results(h_out, \"OutputVector\");\n        }\n\n        // Validation\n        if (validate) {\n            printf(\"Validating result...\\n\");\n            const bool valid = verifyResults(h_reference.data(), h_out.data(), numRows);\n\n            if (valid) {\n                printf(\"Validation: PASSED\\n\");\n            } else {\n                printf(\"Validation: FAILED\\n\");\n                exitCode = 1;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    MPI_Bcast(&exitCode, 1, MPI_INT, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n    MPI_Finalize();\n    return exitCode;\n}\nEOF\ncd {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-5-5","id":"msg_011Cfgh5WHvJGwdCqhHKMqeB","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"`spmv.cpp` and `CMakeLists.txt` now use MPI, OpenMP and CUDA together, and the executable is still `spmv`. Validation passes with 1, 2, 3, 4 and 8 MPI ranks (8 ranks share the 4 GPUs), and also on a tiny 5×5 matrix that has fewer rows than GPUs.\n\n**How the work is split**\n- **MPI:** rank 0 builds the matrix with the same `rand()` sequence as before, so the data is identical. This step stays serial because the random sequence can't be split up. Rank 0 then divides the rows into contiguous blocks with roughly equal work and sends each rank only its own block, plus the full input vector.\n- **GPU assignment:** the GPUs on each node are shared among that node's ranks. If a rank owns several GPUs (for example, one rank on a 4-GPU node), it runs one OpenMP thread per GPU, so all GPUs are used even with `mpirun -np 1`.\n- **CUDA:** a group of threads works on each row, and the group size is picked from the average row length (2 to 64 threads, tuned by measurement). Iterations are batched into CUDA graphs, which cuts launch overhead on small matrices.\n- **OpenMP:** besides driving the GPUs, it parallelises the CPU reference calculation used by `-v`. That calculation still adds each row in the original order, so the reference values are unchanged.\n- **Timing:** the timed section covers all iterations plus copying the results back and collecting them on rank 0. It does not include setup or moving the data to the GPUs, and there is one untimed warm-up run first.\n\n**Results on this machine (4× RTX 3090)**\n\n| Case | Original (serial) | New |\n|---|---|---|\n| n=20000, s=10 | 62.25 ms/iter, 1.3 GFLOPS | 0.14 ms/iter with 4 ranks (~440×); 0.24 ms with 1 rank driving all 4 GPUs |\n| n=40000, s=10 | — | 0.665 ms/iter, ~480 GFLOPS; about 720 GB/s per GPU against the RTX 3090's ~936 GB/s maximum |\n| n=1024, 100k iterations, 1 rank | — | 19 → 56 GFLOPS from the CUDA graphs |\n\n**Things that behave differently**\n- **`-r` hash:** the Sum line printed by `-r` matches the original at n=20000, but the Hash line differs. The GPU adds each row's values in a different order, so the last bits of some results change. This is well inside the 2% tolerance that `-v` checks.\n- **Setup time:** generating the matrix on rank 0 is still serial and is now the slowest part of a large run (about 7 s at n=20000). It isn't counted in the reported time.\n- **Default size (n=1024, 10 iterations):** this finishes in under 1 ms. Because the timer counts whole milliseconds, it can print 0 ms and \"inf GFLOPS\", just as the original would with a run that fast.\n\n**Build changes:** CMake now enables CUDA and requires MPI, OpenMP and the CUDA toolkit. `spmv.cpp` is compiled by nvcc, for the build machine's GPU type by default. You can override that with `-DCMAKE_CUDA_ARCHITECTURES=…`, and the build type defaults to Release. Run it with something like `mpirun -np <ranks> ./spmv -n 20000 -v`."}],"container":null,"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":91,"cache_read_input_tokens":53925,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":91},"output_tokens":3,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"input_transformations":[],"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"0a137d89-0cce-4552-b652-2154f8f07f3f","uuid":"d9e41e73-5a2d-4056-88a0-432bd2dd9e0d","timestamp":"2026-10-04T06:04:21.687Z","request_id":"req_011Cfgh5VyZv8ZtN7CWoPAyU"}
